{"forms":[{"form":"the Herbrand-Ribet theorem","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["A result on the class group of certain number fields, strengthening Ernst Kummer's theorem to the effect that the prime p divides the class number of the cyclotomic field of p-th roots of unity iff p divides the numerator of the n-th Bernoulli number Bₙ for some n, 0 < n < p − 1.
